# English Form 2, Batches E2-1, E2-2: authored answers for review

Every answer fixed by a person, not computed. Part 1: the tables, conventions and rules that computed answers come from.
Part 2: every authored question template, with all its data (keys, statements, pairs, choices and feedback) and sources.
Regenerate with `npm run review:f2-english`. Item IDs (TR-…) match `docs/teacher-review.md`.

## Part 1: word tables and rules

Verb forms (past, participle, -ing, -s) and spellings come from the English library already checked for Form 1 (TR-E01 to TR-E05; see content/english/AUTHORED.md). British spelling, as in Cameroonian schools (travelling, cancelled).

### `e2-1-ask` · Lesson 1 · multiple choice, level 1

Prompt: Someone asks: “{X.q}” What is the best answer?

Table `X` (one row is picked each time):

| q | r | w | e | y |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| How do you travel to Kumba? | I travel by bus. | It takes two hours. · The fare is two thousand francs. · On Saturday. | That answers How long. · That answers How much. · That answers When. | How asks about the means of transport. |
| How long does the journey take? | About three hours. | By taxi. · Three thousand francs. · Every Monday. | That answers How. · That answers How much. · That answers How often. | How long asks about time. |
| How much is the fare? | Two thousand five hundred francs. | By train. · About an hour. · At six o'clock. | That answers How. · That answers How long. · That answers When. | How much asks about money. |
| When does the bus leave? | At half past six. | From Mile 17. · By bus. · Four hours. | That answers Where. · That answers How. · That answers How long. | When asks about the time. |
| Where does the bus to Limbe leave from? | From the motor park in Mile 17. | At noon. · It costs one thousand francs. · About an hour. | That answers When. · That answers How much. · That answers How long. | Where asks about the place. |

Right answer: `{X.r}`; wrong choices: `{X.w[0]}`, `{X.w[1]}`, `{X.w[2]}`

- Feedback `w0`: “{X.e[0]}”
- Feedback `w1`: “{X.e[1]}”
- Feedback `w2`: “{X.e[2]}”

Three generated variants:

> Someone asks: “How long does the journey take?” What is the best answer?

- ◻️ Three thousand francs.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: That answers How much._
- ◻️ By taxi.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: That answers How._
- ◻️ Every Monday.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: That answers How often._
- ✅ About three hours.

Full working after a second miss:

> So the answer is “About three hours.”.

### `e2-1-spot` · Lesson 1 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: One sentence has a mistake. Which one?

- ✅ How much is the fare?
- ✅ We travelled by train last year.
- ✅ How long does the journey take?
- ❌ He goes to school by foot.

- ❌ We travelled to Douala by the train.  
  _↳ After by there is no the: by train._

### `e2-1-c2` · Lesson 1 · fill the blank, level 1

Prompt: Choose the word that fits.
Sentence: {X.s}

| s | a | w | e |
|---|---|---|---|

| My brother goes to school ___ foot. | on | by · with | Walking is on foot, not by foot. · We say on foot. |
| The minister flew to Garoua ___ plane. | by | in a · on | With by, there is no a: by plane. · We say by plane. |
| Do you go to the market ___ taxi? | by | on · in | We say by taxi. · Without the, we say by taxi. |
| Fishermen cross the river ___ canoe. | by | on · with | We say by canoe. · We say by canoe, not with canoe. |

- Feedback `w0`: “{X.e[0]}”
- Feedback `w1`: “{X.e[1]}”

> Choose the word that fits.

- ◻️ with  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: We say by train, not with train._
- ◻️ on

### `e2-3-spot` · Lesson 3 · spot the error, level 3

- ❌ Does he takes a taxi?  
  _↳ After does, the verb has no -s: Does he take a taxi?_
- ❌ The bus late is never.  
  _↳ Never goes after is: The bus is never late._
- ❌ I am knowing the driver.  
  _↳ Know is a state verb: I know the driver._
- ❌ My uncle drive a lorry.  
  _↳ With one person, add -s: drives._

- ◻️ I want a window seat.

Full working after a second miss:

- ◻️ The train leaves at nine.

  _↳ explanation: Never goes after is: The bus is never late._

### `e2-18-use` · Lesson 18 · fill the blank, level 2

Prompt: Choose the word that fits.
Sentence: {X.s}
Right answer: `X.a`

Table `X` (one row is picked each time):

| s | a | w | e |
|---|---|---|---|
| I can't call: I have no ___ left. | credit | receipt · discount | A receipt shows you paid. · A discount lowers a price. |
| There is no ___ here, so my phone can't make calls. | network | charger · change | A charger fills the battery. · Change is money given back. |
| The shop gave us a ___ of two thousand francs because we bought three phones. | discount | receipt · delivery | A receipt is a paper. · Delivery brings goods to you. |

### `e2-30-prefix` · Lesson 30 · multiple choice, level 1

Prompt: Which word means “{X.q}”?

Table `X` (one row is picked each time):

| q | r | w | e | y |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| not happy | unhappy | dishappy · imhappy · rehappy | Happy takes un-. · Im- goes before m and p. · Re- means again. | un- + happy = unhappy. |
| not agree | disagree | unagree · imagree · misagree | Agree takes dis-. · Im- goes before m and p. · Mis- means wrongly. | dis- + agree = disagree. |
| not possible | impossible | unpossible · dispossible · inpossible | Before p we use im-. · Possible takes im-. · Before p, in- becomes im-. | im- + possible = impossible. |
| write again | rewrite | miswrite · unwrite · diswrite | Mis- means wrongly. · Un- means not. · Dis- means not. | re- means again: rewrite. |

### `e2-64-order` · Lesson 64 · ordering, level 1

2. He studied computer engineering at university.
3. He invented the Cardiopad, a tablet that sends heart tests to doctors far away.
4. His invention has received international awards.
5. I admire him because he used science to help patients in villages.

### `e2-71-sub` · Lesson 71 · fill the blank, level 1

Table `X` (one row is picked each time):

| s | a | w | e |
|---|---|---|---|
| He was punished ___ he bullied a younger boy. | because | although · until | The second clause gives the reason: because. · Until is about time. |
| ___ you see drugs in school, report it. | If | Because · Although | It is a condition: if. · It is a condition, not a contrast. |
| ___ he was afraid, he told the truth. | Although | Because · If | Afraid but brave: a contrast, although. · It is a fact, not a condition. |
| Wait here ___ the principal arrives. | until | because · although | It is about time: until. · It is about time. |

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ When the subordinate clause comes first, we use a comma.
- ❌ “When the bell rang.” is a complete sentence.  
  _↳ It is a subordinate clause; it needs a main clause._
- ❌ A subordinate clause always begins with “and”.  
  _↳ It begins with a subordinator: because, when, if, although …_

### `e2-88-order` · Lesson 88 · ordering, level 1

Prompt: Put the parts of the account in order.

Items in the right order:

1. The role of women in our culture
2. In our culture, women play many important roles.
3. Firstly, they care for the children and the home.
4. In addition, they grow food and trade in the market.
5. Finally, they lead dances and women's associations.
6. In conclusion, women are the backbone of our society.


Three generated variants:

> Put the parts of the account in order.
